# Member delta: 2012 Shipping PDB vs reference UE3 10897 headers

Generated by `resources/tools/symbols/member_delta.py` over modules Core, Engine, GameFramework, IpDrv, WinDrv, D3D9Drv, GFxUI, OnlineSubsystemSteamworks, Launch. 2804 classes compared: 1766 identical member lists, 1038 differ. 7610 PDB classes have no reference declaration (Arkane / DishonoredGame).

Textual comparison only (names, not offsets); the compiled offset delta is `reference_layout_delta.md`.
